Motivation: 当前多语言评测只比较最终答案而丢弃动作轨迹，但动作才是系统的真正输出——它决定了成本、延迟、失败模式，也是唯一可审计的行为。作者旨在让动作策略成为被测对象，并揭示朴素测量中隐藏的混杂因素。

Method: 在8个模型、6个平行基准、41种语言上进行2.38M次rollout，将动作策略作为测量对象。识别并逐一消除五个混杂因素（短轨迹、空轨迹、随机一致性、可复现性上限、基线缺失），采用置换检验估计机会下限而非假设，并通过预注册预测验证英语pivot的因果关系。

Result: 每消除一个混杂因素，效应反而更大；发散在贪心解码下仍存在、随温度升高保持平直（尽管自一致性下降）。归一化可复现性后，四个前沿模型在贪心解码下收敛于71-73%的跨语言动作保留率，模型身份仅解释5.7%方差。低于10B参数此规律崩溃，小模型排序多是机会地板的伪影。英语pivot是因果负载的（预注册预测确认），指令无法让其放弃。一个正则表达式可使某模型测量精度提高26倍。

Conclusion: 跨语言动作策略发散是结构性的、非采样噪声，且被模型大小与语言路由机制（英语pivot）所支配。多语言评测必须测量动作策略而非最终答案，且必须进行混杂校正；评测中的提取脚本等工程细节可以产生远大于模型差异的虚假效应。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [172] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ASMI（注意力子网络互信息）作为训练无关的不确定性估计器，通过掩码注意力头并测量子网络间的BALD互信息来捕获"置信但脆弱"的预测，从而补充单一置信度与熵之外的错误预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模型不确定性仅通过输出分布的广度（置信度/熵）来度量，但忽略了预测"脆弱性"——即置信预测在注意力路径扰动下是否不稳定。作者希望捕捉这种互补信号，以提升错误预测的识别能力。

Method: ASMI通过掩码不同注意力头生成子网络，并在这些子网络间计算BALD互信息作为不确定性信号；引入语义一致性核函数以折扣表面形式差异。SM-ASMI变体可从单一贪心解码中读取信号，无需随机生成；自适应变体复用基线已采样的10个样本。

Result: 在有依据QA上，ASMI在置信度/熵之外增加了错误预测信息，对"置信但脆弱"的预测可将置信度过滤器保留的误差大致减半；在12个有依据基准-主干设置中，Sem-ASMI在10个上持平或超过语义熵；最佳ASMI变体在8个上持平或领先最强基线；在参数化QA上如预期退化到MSP基线水平；估计具有近确定性。

Conclusion: 注意力路径的脆弱性是一种与输出置信度正交的不确定性信号，其适用性是可预测的（依领域分级），在答案经由上下文路由时有价值，而在纯参数回忆时无增益。头部级分析表明，关键在于脆弱性是否与错误耦合，而非脆弱性本身。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [173] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多语言场景下的安全对齐问题，聚焦于四种非洲低资源语言（Twi、Hausa、Amharic和Swahili）。通过引入新数据集LoDNA和基于潜在几何空间的拒绝表示探测框架，发现跨语言安全迁移严重受限：有害提示在多数语言-模型对中保留的英语拒绝信号不足10%，表明当前多语言安全对齐是表面的，不支持存在通用、语言无关的伤害流形的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐主要在英语中开发，假设其防护措施能泛化到多语言环境。然而这一假设缺乏充分验证，尤其是在低资源语言中存在明显的安全漏洞。该研究旨在系统地检验跨语言安全迁移的有效性。

Method: 提出LoDNA安全数据集（包含字面翻译和文化本地化提示），并建议一个潜在几何框架，通过探测隐藏状态中的拒绝表示来评估LLM的安全行为，超越传统的生成式评估方法。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示保留的英语拒绝信号在大多数语言-模型对中不足10%。虽然字面和本地化提示在语义上高度对齐（余弦相似度0.95-0.996），但表征在层间漂移，表明模型编码了概念但未将其路由到安全机制中。

Conclusion: 当前多语言安全对齐是浅层的、表面的，本研究为反对通用语言无关伤害流形的假设提供了有力证据，至少在所研究的低资源语言范围内如此。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对TrustNLP研讨会（2021-2026六届共144篇论文）进行系统性综述，将其按六个信任维度分类，发现信任研究方向随生成式AI能力涌现而演变：真实性成为增长最快的维度，公平性最稳定，可解释性呈现U型轨迹（从后验方法衰退到机制可解释性复苏），并为社区提出四个结构性洞见和行动方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可信NLP（TrustNLP）研讨会从2021年8篇论文扩展到2026年41篇，该领域经历了从静态模型的后验可解释性向生成式系统的机制理解和主动控制的范式转变。这种快速增长和方向演变亟需系统性梳理与分类，以理解信任维度如何随模型能力涌现而变化，并为研究社区提供结构化指导。

Method: 对TrustNLP研讨会所有144篇论文进行分类分析，基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust等既有框架构建六个信任维度分类体系；同时与ACL、NAACL、EACL和EMNLP同期约2000篇论文进行跨会议对比，以评估TrustNLP主题分布的领域代表性。

Result: 研究发现：首个高影响力聊天模型的发布同时激活了所有信任维度，而后继模型世代将焦点转向真实性与安全对齐。真实性是增长最快的维度（2021-2022年缺席，2025-2026年占37%）；公平性是最一致的主题；可解释性呈U型轨迹——后验方法衰退后于2026年通过机制可解释性复苏。跨会议对比显示TrustNLP的主题分布紧密跟随领域平均水平。

Conclusion: 研究识别出四个结构性洞见，确认了信任研究方向与模型能力涌现之间存在明显共现关系，并基于分析结果提出了面向研究社区的可行动方向。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConVAWG框架，用于生成针对针对妇女和女孩的暴力（VAWG）场景的合成多轮对话数据。该框架利用检索增强的方法，结合官方犯罪定义、人口统计数据及家庭凶杀案审查案例，生成超过6000条多轮对话事件，并通过激活引导的毒性控制确保生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话数据在敏感领域（如家庭暴力、跟踪、胁迫等）难以获取、发布和标注，且隐私和法律约束限制了大规模真实会话数据的发布。现有研究多聚焦于在线滥用的句子级毒性检测，缺乏将虐待建模为关系性、随时间展开的多轮对话现象的研究。

Method: ConVAWG框架包含以下步骤：(1) 从用户画像种子、英国国家统计局的人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索到的家庭凶杀案审查案例构建场景；(2) 将场景转换为分层事件时间线；(3) 生成多场景角色扮演对话；(4) 对适当的话语应用基于激活引导的毒性控制。共生成200个场景、6000多条多轮对话事件，并附带场景级、事件级和对话轮级的丰富元数据。

Result: 通过广泛的人工评估、LLM作为评审的评估、消融实验和下游任务测试，结果表明ConVAWG生成的对话具有强对话质量和领域保真度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG填补了将虐待建模为关系性、时间上展开的多轮对话现象的空白，为敏感领域对话研究提供了大规模、高质量的合成数据资源，同时通过检索接地和毒性控制保障了数据的可信度与安全性。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Protection Levels for Vision-Based Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10023)
*Olivia Beyer Bruvik,Romeo Valentin,Marc R. Schlichting,Don Walker,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于视觉的跑道位姿估计中的保护级别（Protection Levels）计算方法，为非线性PnP问题在所有六自由度上提供在有未检测故障条件下仍然有效的位姿误差概率边界，并在航空场景中进行了分析与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航作为GNSS的补充手段，其认证要求必须考虑故障测量带来的完整性保障。已有工作提出了基于概率的计算机视觉跑道位姿估计管线和类似接收机自主完整性监测（RAIM）的故障检测方法，但尚未推导保护级别——即在未检测故障下仍有效的位姿误差概率上界。本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 本文在既有概率视觉管线框架基础上，推导保护级别计算方法。具体而言，提出了针对非线性Perspective-n-Point（PnP）问题的保护级别计算算法，直接覆盖飞机位姿的全部六个自由度（位置和姿态）。算法进一步分析了测量冗余度、像素级预测不确定性和跑道距离对保护级别的影响。

Result: 在一个说明性跑道示例上展示了保护级别之间的权衡关系，并定量分析了测量冗余、像素预测不确定性以及跑道距离对保护级别的影响，从而验证了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将RAIM概念中的保护级别扩展至视觉跑道位姿估计的非线性PnP问题，为视觉导航在航空领域的完整性认证提供了一种新的理论工具。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation complements Global Navigation Satellite Systems, but certification demands integrity guarantees that account for faulty measurements. Previous work presented a probabilistic computer vision pipeline for runway-based pose estimation with fault detection inspired by Receiver Autonomous Integrity Monitoring. This work extends that framework by deriving protection levels, which provide probabilistic bounds on pose error that remain valid under undetected faults. We present an algorithm for computing protection levels for the nonlinear Perspective-$n$-Point problem applied to an aviation setting. The algorithm covers all six degrees of freedom of the aircraft pose (position and orientation) directly. We analyze the effect of measurement redundancy, pixel-level prediction uncertainty, and runway distance on the resulting protection levels. To make the results tangible, we demonstrate tradeoffs in the protection levels on an illustrative runway example.

</details>


### [177] [The Impact of Operational-Data Fidelity when Assessing Safety-Critical Autonomous-Vehicle Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10025)
*Kizito Salako,Rabiu Tsoho Muhammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，提出了一种从统计角度检验基于不充分详细的运行数据所推导的软件可靠性声明鲁棒性的方法，并在自动驾驶车辆（AV）安全评估场景中证明了数据细节不足可能使可靠性声明过于乐观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键软件的可靠性评估通常依赖其历史运行数据（如成功和失败事件序列），但这些数据往往缺乏对失败事件的充分细节描述，导致基于这些数据的可靠性评估无法考虑过去失败的重要特征，从而可能产生误导性的可靠性声明。

Method: 扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，用于处理数据细节不足的情况下进行可靠性评估；通过形式化方法检验可靠性声明的鲁棒性，并在自动驾驶车辆安全评估场景中演示评估方法。

Result: 从不够细粒度的数据中推导出的可靠性声明可能危险地过于乐观，即使评估者刻意保守地使用这些数据也是如此。研究给出了数据保真度影响评估的首个保守估计，并澄清了为何尝试保守地使用低保真度数据可能是天真的。

Conclusion: 数据细节不足会严重削弱软件可靠性声明；与先前关于统计模型保真度对贝叶斯软件可靠性评估影响的工作一致，但本研究首次明确解释了为何保守使用低保真度数据的做法可能不够充分，并为数据保真度的影响提供了首个保守估计。该结果对AV安全评估等场景具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: For safety-critical software, data from the software's operational past (e.g. a sequence of success and failure events experienced by the software) can provide strong statistical support for reliability claims about the software. However, such data might not describe past software failure events in sufficient detail, and this might leave a reliability assessment (based on this data) unable to account for important features of past software failures. In this paper, by extending conservative Bayesian inference (CBI) techniques used in reliability assessment, we illustrate a principled statistical approach for checking the robustness of reliability claims derived from insufficiently detailed operational data. We demonstrate the extent to which insufficient detail in operational data can undermine software reliability claims in autonomous vehicle (AV) safety assessment scenarios. Reliability claims derived from insufficiently fine-grained data might be dangerously optimistic, despite a concerted effort by an assessor to use such data conservatively during the assessment. While these findings are consistent with previous work on the impact of statistical model fidelity in Bayesian software reliability assessments, our work clarifies why attempts to use low-fidelity data conservatively can be naive, and we give the first conservative estimates of the impact of data fidelity on assessments.

</details>


### [178] [Navigating the Proximity-Safety Balance: Constraint Decomposition for Human Following in Pedestrian Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10056)
*Shiting Gong,Jianpeng Yao,Jinfeng Wang,Marco Pavone,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多约束强化学习的密集人群跟随方法，将稀疏任务奖励与独立成本约束解耦，并通过量化行人运动预测不确定性来增强安全性，在仿真和真实机器人上验证了接近度-安全性平衡的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集人群中跟随目标存在靠近目标与安全导航之间的固有冲突。现有强化学习方法将竞争目标编码为单一密集奖励，导致邻近-安全平衡隐式且难以跨场景调整，尤其在行人行为不可预测时表现不佳。

Method: 将人类跟随任务分解为稀疏任务奖励和独立成本约束，构建多约束强化学习框架。每个约束通过具有直接行为含义的成本阈值而非隐式奖励权重比进行管理，实现显式可调的权衡控制；同时量化行人运动预测不确定性并将其整合进RL成本，以增强不可预测条件下的安全性。

Result: 在分布内和分布外场景的广泛实验中，该方法相比基线实现了有效的邻近-安全平衡；真实机器人部署进一步验证了方法在实际场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 多约束RL框架配合不确定性量化的行人运动预测，能够显式且可调地控制密集人群跟随中的邻近-安全权衡，兼顾任务完成度与安全性，并具备实际部署能力。

Abstract: Following a target human in crowded environments involves an inherent conflict between staying close to the target and navigating safely among surrounding pedestrians and obstacles. This conflict becomes more severe in dense scenarios, where aggressive following risks collisions and conservative margins lead to target loss, especially when pedestrian behaviors are unfamiliar or unpredictable. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods typically encode these competing objectives into a single dense reward, but the resulting proximity-safety balance is implicit and difficult to adjust across conditions. To address this, we decompose the human-following task into a sparse task reward and independent cost constraints within a multi-constraint RL formulation, where each constraint is managed through cost thresholds with direct behavioral meaning rather than implicit reward weight ratios, allowing explicit and tunable control over the trade-off. We further quantify the prediction uncertainty of human motions and integrate these estimates into the RL costs to enhance safety under unpredictable conditions. Extensive experiments across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings demonstrate that our method achieves an effective proximity-safety balance compared to baselines. Real-robot deployment further validates the feasibility of our method in real-world scenarios. More details are available on our project page: https://nav-ps-balance.github.io/.

</details>


### [179] [Whole-Body Planning for Humanoids Navigating Confined Spaces via Self-Collision Avoidance References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10220)
*Carlos Gonzalez,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种三阶段全身规划框架，直接在运动学可达的刚体体积上进行路径规划，结合可微碰撞避免与可达性约束，生成体积感知的引导轨迹来驱动全阶轨迹优化器，并训练残差强化学习策略实现鲁棒的在线执行，在Unitree G1人形机器人高受限环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在高度受限环境中的运动需要同时处理密集环境障碍、复杂自碰撞约束和多接触动态可行性。传统轨迹优化器在此类受限空间中表现不佳，因为基于粒子抽象的样条在大碰撞空间中的导航能力不足，容易陷入较差的局部极小值。

Method: 提出三阶段全身规划框架：(1) 直接在运动学可达的刚体体积上进行运动学路径规划；(2) 将可微碰撞避免集成到可达性约束的优化公式中，合成体积感知引导轨迹，用于引导全阶轨迹优化器在长时间跨度上可靠地工作；(3) 将优化后的计划作为高质量参考，训练残差强化学习策略以实现鲁棒的在线执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上通过三个基准测试平台验证，超过NIST应急响应标准，实现了受限空间比(Cr < 1.5)；在12至18秒的复杂任务中生成了具有复杂手部和脚部接触的可执行轨迹（标准基线方法失效）；学习到的策略在广泛领域随机化下成功跟踪这些计划。

Conclusion: 该框架通过体积感知的路径规划与轨迹优化的结合，有效解决了高受限环境下人形机器人运动规划的难题；优化的计划可作为高质量参考来训练残差强化学习策略，兼顾规划的可行性与执行层的鲁棒性，推动了人形机器人在极端受限环境中的实际部署能力。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion in highly confined environments requires navigating dense environmental obstacles and complex self-collision bounds while maintaining multi-contact dynamic feasibility. Traditional trajectory optimizers frequently struggle in these restricted spaces, as navigating the large collision space with splines on particle abstractions is insufficient and leads to poor local minima. To address this, we propose a three-stage whole-body planning framework that formulates kinematic path planning directly over kinematically reachable rigid-body volumes. By integrating differentiable collision avoidance into a reachability-constrained formulation, our framework synthesizes volume-informed guides that reliably guide a full-order trajectory optimizer over long horizons. We show that these optimized plans serve as high-quality references to train a residual reinforcement learning policy for robust online execution. We validate our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid across three benchmark testbeds exceeding NIST emergency response standards, achieving restricted confinement ratios ($C_r < 1.5$). Our framework generates feasible trajectories across 12-to-18-second tasks with complex foot and hand contacts where standard baselines fail, while the learned policy successfully tracks these plans under extensive domain randomization in physics simulation.

</details>


### [180] [FACT: Failure-Aware Causal Training for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10232)
*Quanquan Peng,Yutong Liang,Rui Yan,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FACT是一种因果世界-行动模型（causal World-Action Model），它通过将未来视频预测和任务进度预测与所执行的动作条件化，使模型能够从失败轨迹中学习动作后果，从而缓解现有世界行动模型中的成功偏差问题，在仿真和真实双臂操作任务上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界行动模型（WAMs）大多仅在成功演示上训练，缺乏对糟糕动作后果的预测能力。这使得模型在遇到失败动作时会产生有偏的未来想象，无法提供正确的物理先验用于动作生成。

Method: 提出FACT，一种因果世界-行动模型，它同时预测未来视频和任务进度，并将二者与所执行的动作进行条件化。通过引入动作条件接口，让失败轨迹也参与监督动作后果，将坏动作转化为有效的未来预测目标而非丢弃；失败感知训练使进度预测器同时感知成功和失败的动作结果，推理时可选地用于对采样动作候选进行评分。

Result: 在仿真和真实双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，FACT优于多种现有基线；随着失败数据加入训练，性能持续提升；在坏动作条件下能够减少成功偏置的未来幻觉。

Conclusion: 利用失败数据来监督动作后果是提升世界行动模型泛化能力和物理先验质量的有效途径，动作条件下的因果建模为世界模型提供了更全面的学习信号。

Abstract: Recent world-action models (WAMs) show that co-training policies with future prediction can provide physical priors for action generation. Building on the future-prediction ability of video models, many WAMs generate future videos and recover actions with inverse-dynamics models, or use these predicted videos as goal conditions for action generation. In both cases, the world model is trained mostly on successful demonstrations and has little reason to predict the consequences of bad actions. We introduce FACT, a causal World-Action Model that predicts future video and task progress conditioned on the executed action. This action-conditioned interface allows failure rollouts to supervise action consequences, turning bad actions into valid future targets rather than being discarded. Failure-aware training makes the progress predictor aware of both successful and failed action outcomes, which can optionally be used to score sampled action candidates at inference. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that FACT outperforms many existing baselines, improves as failure data are incorporated into training, and reduces success-biased future hallucination under bad actions. See more details at https://fact-wam.github.io/

</details>


### [181] [A Neural Network Based Teleoperation for Remote Controlled Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10367)
*Ning Ding,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向直接车辆遥操作的单边遥操作框架，结合波变量（WV）方法保证随机时延下的被动稳定性，并采用自适应径向基函数网络（RBFN）在线补偿车辆动力学不确定性。与现有面向双边机械臂的WV-神经网络架构不同，该框架为车辆纵横向动力学设计了解耦的自适应律。仿真对比PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC表明RBFN在抗未建模扰动方面更优，且执行时间比MPC/NMPC低数个数量级；基于1/10比例车辆和4G网络的硬件在环实验验证了系统在物理道路不确定性下的实际可行性、安全性和轨迹跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接遥操作车辆面临两大核心瓶颈：通信时延导致的不稳定性，以及操作者无法物理感知未建模环境扰动（如气动阻力、道路横坡角）和高度非线性的轮胎-路面动力学。

Method: 构建单边遥操作框架，包含两层补偿：(1) 波变量（WV）方法被动保证随机时延下的系统稳定性；(2) 自适应径向基函数网络（RBFN）主动补偿车辆特定不确定性。关键创新在于为车辆纵横向动力学设计了解耦的自适应律，不同于现有面向双边机械臂的WV-神经网络架构，且RBFN为无模型方法，避免了预测控制器的高计算开销。

Result: 仿真对比表明RBFN在抗未建模扰动方面优于PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC，执行时间较MPC/NMPC低数个数量级，适合资源受限的车辆边缘计算场景；1/10比例车辆在4G网络下的硬件在环实验验证了系统的实际可行性、安全性和鲁棒轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的WV+RBFN单边遥操作框架有效解决了通信时延和未建模扰动两大问题，兼顾稳定性保障与实时自适应能力，为远程车辆操控提供了计算高效且实践可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Direct teleoperation of vehicles faces critical technical bottlenecks: communication latency and the operator's inability to physically perceive unmodeled environmental disturbances (e.g., aerodynamic drag, bank angles) coupled with highly nonlinear tire-road dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose a tailored unilateral teleoperation framework. The system integrates the Wave Variable (WV) approach to passively guarantee stability under stochastic delays, and an adaptive Radial Basis Function Network (RBFN) to actively compensate for vehicle-specific uncertainties. Unlike existing WV-neural network architectures designed for bilateral robotic arms, our framework features decoupled adaptive laws specifically designed for vehicle longitudinal and lateral dynamics. Furthermore, compared to model-heavy predictive controllers, the model-free RBFN offers rapid online adaptation without heavy computational overhead. Building upon our preliminary theoretical formulation, this brief paper presents comprehensive comparative analyses and real-world hardware validations. Simulation benchmarks against PID, LQR, MPC, and NMPC demonstrate that the RBFN achieves superior robustness against unmodeled disturbances while requiring orders of magnitude less execution time than MPC and NMPC, making it ideal for resource-constrained vehicle edge computing. Finally, hardware-in-the-loop experiments using a 1/10th scale vehicle over a 4G network validate the system's practical feasibility, safety, and robust trajectory tracking under physical road uncertainties.

</details>


### [182] [Real-World Cooperative Bimanual Dexterous Grasp of Large Objects from Single-View Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10383)
*Ziming Li,Mingxuan Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hongfei Li,Yan Gan,Deqiang Ouyang,Ning Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向真实世界的双臂灵巧抓取大物体的框架，包含多模态数据集、基于DDPM的抓取配置生成模块以及结合运动规划与在线抓取细化的执行策略，可从单视角输入合成可执行的双臂抓取，减少对完整3D模型的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧抓取大物体是机器人操作中的关键挑战，但现有研究多集中于顺序操作而非协同抓取，且相关方法大多局限于仿真环境。其根本原因在于获取完整3D物体模型困难以及生成物理上合理的抓取动作难度大。

Method: 提出一个真实世界双臂抓取框架，包括三个核心组件：(1) 多模态数据集，捕捉关节角度、视觉观察和力信号；(2) 基于去噪扩散概率模型(DDPM)的模块，从分割点云生成关节级抓取配置；(3) 集成运动规划与在线抓取细化的执行策略，确保物理稳定性和可行性。

Result: 在双臂机器人上的实验表明，该方法在包含不同几何形状和姿态的未见物体上取得了较高的成功率；消融实验验证了系统关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法能从单视角输入合成可执行的双臂抓取，减少对完整3D物体模型的依赖，同时保证真实世界中的稳定性能，填补了双臂协同抓取从仿真走向真实应用的空白。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping of large objects is a critical challenge in robotic manipulation. However, most existing studies focus on sequential manipulation rather than cooperative grasping, and methods addressing such bimanual tasks have largely been limited to simulation. These limitations stem from the difficulty of acquiring full 3D object models and generating physically plausible grasping actions. To fill this gap, we propose a real-world bimanual grasping framework that includes: a multimodal dataset capturing joint angles, visual observations and force signals; a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based module that generates joint-level grasp configurations from segmented point clouds; and an execution strategy that integrates motion planning with online grasp refinement to ensure physical stability and feasibility. Our approach enables the synthesis of executable bimanual grasps from single-view inputs, reducing dependence on complete 3D object models and ensuring stable real-world performance. Experiments on a dual-arm robot demonstrate high success rates across unseen objects with varying geometries and poses, and ablation studies confirm the contributions of key components of our system.

</details>


### [183] [Hip Energized Monopedal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10387)
*Shane Rozen-Levy,Griffon McMahon,Daniel Koditschek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对俯仰未锁定的平面单足机器人的新型步态策略，通过移动质心位置增强俯仰稳定力矩来补偿阻尼带来的能量损失，并设计新的落足策略在径向和角向自由度间分配能量，最终在Penn Jerboa物理机器人上实现1.02-1.77 m/s的稳定运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俯仰未锁定的平面单足机器人中，传统PD加前馈控制器在稳定俯仰时产生的反作用力矩通常被视为需要抑制的干扰，本文的动机在于开创新地利用这些反作用力矩来补充步态中的能量损失，从而无需额外能量输入即可维持稳定运动。

Method: 1) 通过移动质心位置增大俯仰稳定力矩以向步态注入能量；2) 提出新的落足策略来调节能量在径向和角向自由度之间的分配，以抵消耗散损失并实现用户指定的稳态前进速度与腾空高度之间的平衡；3) 采用混合平均分析推导步态不动点和特征值的闭式表达式。

Result: 混合平均分析给出了物理参数和控制参数对性能影响的闭式数学描述；仿真研究在通用5连杆双足模型和精细的Penn Jerboa模型上验证了与解析预测的一致性；物理实验在Penn Jerboa上实现了1.02 m/s至1.77 m/s（相当于5.10至8.85腿长/秒）的稳定运动，且实验行为与数学分析预测有效吻合。

Conclusion: 该研究证明俯仰稳定力矩可以被主动招募用于能量补偿，为能量效率更高的单足/双足机器人步态控制提供了新思路；闭式分析与实验的高度一致性表明该控制框架具有良好的可预测性和可移植性。

Abstract: We present a novel stepping strategy for pitch unlocked planar monopeds where the reaction torques from stabilizing pitch with a conventional PD + feedfoward controller are recruited to counteract energetic losses from damping. By moving the location of the mass center, our controller increases the pitch stabilization torque, thereby adding energy to the gait. A new stepping policy adjusts the distribution of energy between the radial and angular degrees of freedom to counteract dissipative losses and achieve a user specified balance between steady state fore-aft speed and apex height. Hybrid averaging analysis yields closed form expressions for the fixed points and eigenvalues of the resulting gait, lending insight into the interplay between the physical and control parameters' influence on performance. Simulation studies on a generic 5 link biped and a careful model of the Penn Jerboa reveal a useful correspondence to these analytical predictions. Physical experiments on the Penn Jerboa exhibit stable locomotion with speeds ranging from 1.02 m/s to 1.77 m/s (5.10 leg lengths/s to 8.85 leg lengths/s) in a manner effectively approximated by the mathematical analysis.

</details>


### [184] [PBD-AG: Persistent Baseline-Delta Active Graphs with Uncertainty-Aware Inspection for Long-Horizon Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10449)
*Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Shuyue Zhang,Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Han Yu,Chengjie Xu,Yiheng Bi,Kai Sun,Fuchun Sun,Xinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PBD-AG提出了一种持久基线-动态活动图框架，将机器人验证的稳定固定设施与可修订的动态物体事件解耦，通过自主引导基线构建和层级物体信念接地，实现对长时间跨度服务机器人的可追踪世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间跨度服务机器人需要持久的、可自主构建并能随任务相关物体变化而修订的世界模型。现有方法存在定位与观测误差累积、无法捕捉物体持续变化，或缺乏可验证3D几何证据等问题。

Method: PBD-AG框架解耦机器人验证的稳定固定设施与可修订的动态物体事件；机器人通过机载探索自主引导结构基线并检查发现固定设施以接地层级物体信念；维持可靠性加权物体状态（几何、语义、身份、存在、支撑关系）；利用几何可见性门控缓解遮挡下的错误删除；通过图条件策略选择检查视点，平衡目标覆盖、旅行成本、碰撞风险和冗余观测。

Result: 在多种环境和受控动态评估下的模拟实验显示，聚合粗略固定设施F1得分高于能力匹配的对照组，且身份连续性和事件召回更强；物理机器人定性演示展示了与机载感知的集成，为长周期机器人感知提供了可追踪世界模型。

Conclusion: PBD-AG通过基线-动态解耦和可靠性加权状态维护，为长时间跨度机器人感知提供了一种可验证、可追踪且可自主更新的持久世界模型框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon service robots require persistent world models that can be built autonomously in unseen environments and revised as task-relevant objects change. Existing methods rely on online mapping, which accumulates localization and observation errors, static scene representations that cannot capture persistent object changes, or holistic vision-language predictions that lack verifiable 3D geometric evidence. We present PBD-AG, a persistent baseline-delta active graph framework that decouples robot-verified stable fixtures from revisable dynamic object events. Under our framework, the robot autonomously bootstraps the structural baseline from onboard exploration and inspects discovered fixtures to ground hierarchical object beliefs. PBD-AG maintains reliability-weighted object states over geometry, semantics, identity, existence, and support relations, utilizing a geometric visibility gate to mitigate false deletions under occlusion. Inspection viewpoints are selected by a graph-conditioned policy that balances target coverage, travel cost, collision risk, and redundant observation. Simulation experiments in multiple environments and under controlled dynamic evaluation show higher aggregate coarse-fixture F1 than capability-matched controls, as well as stronger identity continuity and event recall. A qualitative physical-robot demonstration further illustrates integration with onboard sensing, providing a traceable world model for long-horizon robotic perception.The project page of PBD-AG is available at https://shuobao214.github.io/PBD-AG/

</details>


### [185] [Lost in Reconstruction: Aligning Action Representations with Language in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10484)
*Li Wenjie,Yash Jangir,Ignacy Stepka,Yash Agarwal,Marion Kipsang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT（语义对齐动作分词器），通过在VQ-VAE风格的动作分词器中引入辅助目标——让冻结的视觉-语言模型从量化后的动作潜在表示中恢复指令文本——来保留动作轨迹中的动词理解信息。在SimplerEnv中SALT达到71.9%的平均成功率，远超仅重建的VQ-VAE（42.7%）和FAST（31.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作动词不仅描述动作的物理结果，还描述动作执行方式。然而，现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）通常在原始动作空间使用L1/L2损失进行重建优化，数值接近度并不反映语言上有意义的区分。在BridgeV2数据上，动作轨迹包含超越视觉状态变化的动词理解信息，而仅重建的离散分词会系统性地侵蚀这些信息，导致语言条件控制性能下降。

Method: SALT在VQ-VAE风格的分词器基础上，增加一个辅助目标：要求一个冻结的视觉-语言模型（VLM）从量化后的动作潜在表示中恢复章节指令（episode instruction）。这迫使动作分词器在保持重建保真度的同时，将语言语义信息（尤其是动词相关信息）编码进离散码本，从而保留动词理解信息。

Result: 在SimplerEnv基准上，SALT训练的模型达到71.9%的平均成功率，显著优于仅重建的VQ-VAE分词器（42.7%）和FAST（31.2%）。此外，SALT发展的动词专用码（verb-specialized codes）表明其成功地将语义结构编码进动作表示，同时保持了重建保真度。

Conclusion: 机器人动作轨迹是语言理解（特别是动词理解）的一个来源，在动作表示中保留这种语言结构可以显著提升语言条件下的机器人控制性能。该工作强调了动作表示设计中语义对齐的重要性，为VLA中的分词器设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Action verbs describe not only the physical outcomes of actions, but also how those actions are performed. Yet action representations in vision-language-action models (VLAs) are typically optimized for reconstruction under L1/L2 losses in raw action space, where numerical proximity need not reflect linguistically meaningful distinctions. On BridgeV2, we show that action trajectories contain verb-grounding information beyond visual state changes, and that reconstruction-only discrete tokenization systematically erodes this information. To address this problem, we introduce SALT, a Semantically ALigned action Tokenizer that augments a VQ-VAE-style tokenizer with an auxiliary objective requiring a frozen vision-language model to recover the episode instruction from quantized action latents. Policies trained with SALT achieve 71.9% average success in SimplerEnv, compared with 42.7% for a reconstruction-only VQ-VAE tokenizer and 31.2% for FAST. SALT also develops verb-specialized codes while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. These results show that robot action trajectories provide a source of language grounding and that preserving this structure in action representations can substantially improve language-conditioned control.

</details>


### [186] [JitTrack: Onboard Multi-Object Tracking Against Viewpoint Jitter for Agile UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10485)
*Yachun Shan,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JitTrack是一个面向敏捷无人机的主动机载多目标跟踪框架，通过语义细化、运动感知查询矫正和运动启发去噪训练来应对相机自运动引起的视角抖动，并构建了感知-规划-控制闭环管线实现真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机多目标跟踪方法主要在离线基准上评估，未充分考虑真实机载部署的实际需求，包括对相机运动的鲁棒性和主动目标跟随能力。敏捷无人机在飞行中剧烈的姿态变化导致跨帧目标大幅位移，引发目标关联不准确和跟踪性能下降。

Method: JitTrack基于查询式Transformer跟踪器构建，包含三个核心组件：(1)语义细化模块用于提升新出现目标的检测能力；(2)运动感知查询矫正模块补偿视角抖动导致的目标错位；(3)运动启发去噪训练策略模拟相机运动模式进行鲁棒监督。此外还开发了感知-规划-控制闭环跟踪管线，实现碰撞避免和物理可行的目标跟随。

Result: 在公开UAV MOT基准上的大量实验表明该方法相比基线有一致性能提升；真实世界飞行实验验证了JitTrack在视角抖动下进行鲁棒机载视觉跟踪的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: JitTrack通过结合运动感知的跟踪算法设计与感知-规划-控制闭环系统，有效解决了敏捷无人机在视角抖动下的多目标跟踪问题，为真实机载部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) onboard agile unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging due to severe viewpoint jitter induced by camera ego-motion. Rapid attitude changes during flight often lead to significant target displacement across frames, causing inaccurate target association and degraded tracking performance. Existing UAV MOT methods are primarily evaluated on offline benchmarks and seldom address the practical requirements of real-world onboard deployment, including robustness to camera motion and active target following. To address these challenges, we propose JitTrack, an active onboard multi-object tracking framework that accommodates drone dynamics and camera ego-motion. Built upon a query-based transformer tracker, JitTrack introduces semantic refinement to improve the detection of emerging targets, motion-aware query rectification to compensate for target misalignment caused by viewpoint jitter, and a motion-inspired denoising training strategy that simulates camera motion patterns for robust supervision. Furthermore, we develop a perception-planning-control closed-loop tracking pipeline for real-world deployment, enabling collision-free and physically feasible target following on agile UAVs. Extensive experiments on public UAV MOT benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over the baseline method, while real-world flight experiments validate the effectiveness and practicality of JitTrack for robust onboard visual tracking under viewpoint jitter.

</details>


### [187] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control via Sequential Convex Programming for Drone-to-Drone Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10542)
*Neeraj Balachandar,Shriram Hari,Vishnu R. Unni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于序贯凸规划（SCP）的滚动时域轨迹优化框架，用于多旋翼飞行器在扰动驱动的目标运动下进行自主空中对接，通过降阶非线性模型、扰动状态增广和状态估计集成，在MuJoCo高保真仿真中验证了其可靠性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中对接任务中，目标受到扰动驱动运动，这构成了一个有约束的非线性轨迹优化难题。现有方法难以同时处理动态可行性、几何捕获约束、估计不确定性和外部扰动，亟需一种鲁棒且计算可行的对接轨迹生成方案。

Method: 将对接任务建模为基于降阶非线性模型并增广扰动状态的有限时域最优控制问题；采用序贯凸规划（SCP）在滚动时域（receding-horizon）框架内求解，生成动态可行的对接轨迹；结合噪声测量下的状态估计实现相对运动预测；在高保真刚体MuJoCo仿真环境中验证轨迹执行。

Result: 在固定目标和匀速运动目标下均可靠收敛至对接接口并满足几何捕获约束；对接锥违例可忽略，终端状态误差在预设公差内；在锥半角低至10度时仍保持一致且安全的对接性能；在风速标准差达0.5的扰动下保持鲁棒运行，同时保证有界接近速度和稳定控制量。

Conclusion: SCP-based轨迹优化框架在估计不确定性下实现了扰动鲁棒的空中对接，验证了该方法在实际任务中的有效性和工程适用性。

Abstract: Autonomous mid-air docking of multi-rotor vehicles under disturbance-driven target motion poses a constrained non-linear trajectory optimization challenge. This work formulates the docking task as a finite-horizon optimal control problem based on a reduced-order nonlinear model augmented with disturbance states. The resulting problem is solved using sequential convex programming within a receding-horizon framework to generate dynamically feasible docking trajectories. State estimation with noisy measurements is incorporated to enable robust relative motion prediction, while trajectory execution is validated in a high-fidelity rigid-body MuJoCo simulation environment. The proposed framework is evaluated for stationary and constant-velocity target motions, demonstrating reliable convergence to the docking interface while satisfying geometric capture constraints. Quantitatively, the method maintains negligible docking-cone violations and terminal state errors within prescribed tolerances, and achieves consistent, safe docking performance for cone half-angles as low as 10 degrees. Robust operation is observed for wind disturbance levels up to a standard deviation of 0.5, while preserving bounded approach velocities and stable control effort. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the SCP-based trajectory optimization framework for disturbance-robust aerial docking under estimation uncertainty.

</details>


### [188] [BooST: Bridging Semantics and Motions for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10600)
*Jusuk Lee,Daesol Cho,Jonghun Shin,Seungyeon Yoo,Jonghae Park,Taekbeom Lee,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BooST，一个两阶段框架，通过跨模态VQ-VAE统一捕获语义意图与运动动力学，并将该表示蒸馏为轻量级策略，从而在少量样本适应、跨域技能迁移和视觉扰动鲁棒性方面取得优异表现，适用于真实机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人技能抽象方法通常只满足"高层语义意图（做什么）"或"底层运动动力学（怎么做）"中的某一子集，导致技能迁移不完整、先验较弱，下游自适应需要大量域内数据。作者希望通过桥接语义与运动来同时满足泛化、鲁棒性和高效性三个目标。

Method: BooST采用两阶段框架：(1) 利用跨模态VQ-VAE同时编码语义意图和运动动力学，构建统一的技能表示；(2) 将该表示蒸馏为轻量级策略，用于高效的下游任务自适应。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，BooST在少样本适应、跨域技能迁移以及对动态视觉干扰物的鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，同时保持了轻量且表达能力强的模型设计，适合真实部署。

Conclusion: BooST通过显式桥接语义与运动两个层面的技能信息，有效解决了现有技能抽象方法覆盖不全面、先验薄弱的问题，为真实机器人上的高效技能迁移与部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Skill abstraction---the process of learning reusable and temporally extended behaviors---has emerged as a key paradigm for improving sample efficiency and generalization in robot learning. For efficient skill transfer to real robots, learned skills must generalize across tasks and domains, remain robust to visual and dynamic perturbations, and be efficient enough for practical deployment. However, existing methods typically satisfy only a subset of these properties, as they capture either high-level semantic intent (what) or low-level motion dynamics (how). This incomplete skill transfer yields weak priors for policy learning, thereby demanding substantial in-domain data for downstream adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce BooST, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges semantics and motions to satisfy all three desiderata. BooST first leverages a cross-modal VQ-VAE to capture both semantic intent and motion dynamics, yielding a unified skill representation. It then distills this representation into a lightweight policy for efficient downstream adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-robot settings demonstrate that BooST achieves superior few-shot adaptation, cross-domain skill transfer, and robustness to dynamic visual distractors, while maintaining a lightweight yet expressive design suitable for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [189] [Toward the Cognitive--Physical Limits of Embodied Intelligence through a World-Model-Centric Autonomous Racing Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10618)
*Zitong Shan,Baichuan Lou,Yanxin Zhou,Shuge Wu,Xianqi He,Bolin Zhao,Sheng Zhao,Zhouheng Li,Chee Kiong Ong,King Ho Holden Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以世界模型为中心的具身智能体，用于自主赛车场景，通过闭环优化联合建模和细化智能体的认知与物理极限，实现高速（256.3 km/h）极限工况下的高交互成功率（88.3%）与多场景泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数具身智能系统在保守安全边际或中等交互条件下评估，其在极端条件下的能力边界缺乏理解。自主赛车提供了结合高频感知、对抗交互、近饱和动力学和严格安全约束的严苛测试环境，但现有系统很少联合建模和细化认知与物理极限。

Method: 提出世界模型为中心的自主赛车智能体框架，从近极限成功与失败中学习预测性世界模型，刻画交互演化、自车动力学和可行运动边界，将世界状态构建、未来感知推理和近极限控制耦合在闭环细化过程中。真实车辆数据收集速度达256.3 km/h，峰值侧向加速度26.8 m/s²。

Result: 在全尺寸模拟赛车的多种挑战性场景中，训练良好的世界模型中心智能体实现88.3%的交互成功率；闭环细化进一步提升了认知-物理极限的利用、故障模式恢复能力以及跨条件与未知赛道的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出一种边界感知方法论，世界模型帮助具身智能体表征、预测并持续细化其能力边界，从而为更安全的真实世界部署提供支持。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence aims to develop agents that perceive, reason, and act through continuous interaction with the physical world. However, most embodied systems are still evaluated within conservative safety margins or moderate interaction regimes, leaving their capability boundaries under extreme conditions insufficiently understood. Autonomous racing provides a stringent testbed by combining high-frequency localization and perception, adversarial interaction, near-saturated vehicle dynamics, and strict safety constraints. Existing systems push high-speed performance but rarely model and refine cognitive and physical limits jointly. Here we show that a world-model-centric autonomous racing agent provides a concrete step toward exploring these coupled limits. The framework learns predictive world models from near-limit successes and failures to capture interaction evolution, ego dynamics, and feasible-motion boundaries, coupling world-state construction, future-aware reasoning, and near-limit control in a closed-loop refinement process. Training data were collected from real-vehicle autonomous racing, where the onboard system maintained robust localization and perception at speeds up to 256.3 km/h and peak lateral acceleration of 26.8 m/s$^2$. In full-scale simulated racing, the well trained world-model-centric agent achieves an 88.3% interaction success rate across various challenging simulated racing scenarios. Closed-loop refinement of the world model and policy further improved utilization of cognitive-physical limits, recovery from failure modes, and generalization across varying conditions and unseen circuits. These results suggest a boundary-aware methodology in which world models help embodied agents represent, predict, and continually refine their capability boundaries for safer real-world deployment.

</details>


### [190] [When Your State Estimator Has Lost The Plot: Detecting Estimator Failures Via Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10623)
*Christian Lanegger,Helen Oleynikova,Roland Siegwart,Michael Pantic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种传感器无关的频域内省方法，通过分析近期速度估计的频域功率分布来评估机器人状态估计器的健康状况。使用无人机飞行数据（视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性、雷达-惯性里程计）进行验证，可在三种不同的状态估计框架中检测出51%-58%的已标注故障，精确率为60%-84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的状态估计对机器人安全运行至关重要，但未建模干扰（如传感器混叠或分布外噪声）会导致估计器退化甚至完全失效。现有不确定性度量（如协方差）依赖理想化假设且容易过于自信，而基于数据驱动的方法又受限于训练数据分布，缺乏轻量且通用的估计质量评估机制。

Method: 提出一种传感器无关的内省方法，对近期速度估计的频域功率分布进行分析，提取信号功率、频谱带宽和熵等频域指标，用于区分健康与退化的估计器输出。方法在三种不同的里程计框架（视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性、雷达-惯性）上使用包含多次估计器故障的真实户外飞行数据集进行评估。

Result: 在三种根本不同的状态估计框架中，观察到健康与退化估计之间存在一致的频谱功率差异，能够检测出51%-58%的已标注故障，精确率为60%-84%。

Conclusion: 简单的频域分析即可作为轻量级的内省工具，补充现有的鲁棒性技术，在真实机器人部署中具有实用价值，并为未来研究提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reliable onboard state estimation is essential for safe robotic operation, yet unmodeled disturbances, such as sensor aliasing or out-of-distribution noise, still cause estimators to degrade or fail completely. While many methods aim to improve estimator robustness, only a few provide introspective mechanisms to assess estimate quality. Existing uncertainty measures, such as covariances, rely on idealized assumptions and tend to be overconfident, and more recent data-driven approaches are typically tied to their training data distributions.
  We propose a sensor-agnostic introspective method that assesses estimator health by analyzing the frequency-domain power distribution of recent velocity estimates. The method is evaluated using outdoor flight data from an aerial robot running visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, and radar-inertial odometry. The dataset includes multiple estimator failures, enabling analysis of several frequency-domain indicators, such as signal power, spectral bandwidth, and entropy. We observe consistent spectral power differences between healthy and degraded estimates, allowing detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures with 60%-84% precision across three fundamentally different state estimation frameworks. Our results show that even a simple frequency-domain analysis of a state estimator's output can serve as a lightweight introspective tool to complement existing robustness techniques in real-world robotic deployments, and opens promising avenues for future investigation.

</details>


### [191] [OAA: Three Phases of Vocal Guidance in Human-Drone Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10651)
*Allan Henry,Christian Graff,Solange Rossato,José-Ernesto Gomez-Balderas,Sylvain Huet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过两种实验配置（人-人手势引导和无人机遥控）的运动捕捉和语音数据，发现自发言语引导始终组织为三个运动学和语言学上截然不同的阶段：定向（Orientation）、接近（Approach）和调整（Adjustment），并通过变点检测和统计验证证实了这一三阶段结构的稳健性，为语音引导遥操作中的自适应控制提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音控制机器人系统将口语命令视为静态数据流，忽略了引导者沟通行为随任务推进而动态演变的事实，缺乏对交互阶段结构的理解。

Method: 采用运动捕捉和语音数据，在两种实验配置（手指指点的真人引导N=10对，游戏手柄控制的无人机遥操作N=29对）中采集数据；通过3D轨迹信号上的变点检测（change point detection）自动识别阶段，并以Kruskal-Wallis检验（p<.001）进行统计验证；同时分析词汇家族、停顿等语言特征。

Result: 自发语音引导一致地组织为定向、接近、调整三个运动学和语言学上不同的阶段；三个词汇家族（旋转词汇标记定向阶段、平移词汇在定向阶段稀缺、衰减词在调整阶段累积）跨配置复现；停顿与这些线索共同标记了语音速率单独无法标记的定向阶段边界；尽管两种配置的运动接口截然不同，三阶段结构仍然一致出现。

Conclusion: 三阶段结构是人类空间引导的内在属性而非实验假象，该发现对语音引导遥操作中的OAA感知自适应控制设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Voice-guided teleoperation requires systems that adapt to the evolving dynamics of human guidance. Yet most voice-controlled robot systems treat spoken commands as a stationary stream, ignoring how the guide's communicative behavior changes as the task progresses. Using motion capture and speech data from two experimental configurations, humanhuman guidance (finger pointing, N =10 dyads) and humandrone teleoperation (gamepad control, N =29 dyads), we show that spontaneous vocal guidance consistently organizes into three kinematically and linguistically distinct phases: Orientation, Approach, and Adjustment. These phases are identified automatically via change point detection on 3D trajectory signals, and validated statistically (Kruskal-Wallis, p<.001). Three lexical families replicate across configurations: rotation vocabulary marks Orientation, translation vocabulary is scarce there, and attenuators accumulate toward Adjustment. Together with inter-utterance silence, these cues mark the Orientation boundary that speech rate alone leaves unmarked. The same three-phase structure emerges in both configurations despite radically different motor interfaces, suggesting it is an intrinsic property of human spatial guidance rather than an artifact of the experimental setup. We discuss implications for OAA-aware adaptive control in voice-guided teleoperation.

</details>


### [192] [Robust Sliding Mode and Admittance Control of Underactuated Aerial Manipulators for Contact-Based Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10656)
*Tareq Aziz Alqutami,Yvan Petillot,Matthew W. Dunnigan,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于欠驱动六旋翼+1自由度机械臂的稳健接触式工业检测控制框架，通过积分增强滑模控制(SMC)与导纳控制的结合，实现了稳定接触力调节。软件在环仿真表明该方法在轨迹追踪和耦合抑制上优于传统PID，力调节RMSE仅0.12N，可稳定施加高达20N接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式工业检测要求空中平台在抑制扰动的同时保持稳定交互，而欠驱动空中机械臂因飞行器姿态与力生成之间的动态耦合存在控制难题，需要设计更鲁棒的控制策略。

Method: 提出一个集成框架：采用积分增强滑模控制(SMC)进行轨迹追踪，结合导纳控制律实现力调节；将接触力映射为前馈姿态项，同时用1自由度机械臂主动补偿倾角以维持表面对齐。

Result: 软件在环仿真表明SMC方法在轨迹追踪和耦合抑制方面优于传统PID；交互策略实现精确力调节，RMSE为0.12N，可稳定施加高达20N的力。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制框架在欠驱动空中机械臂接触式检测场景中表现出高效性，验证了系统在稳定、可靠接触式检测应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Contact-based industrial inspection requires aerial platforms to maintain stable interaction while rejecting disturbances. Underactuated aerial manipulators present control challenges due to the dynamic coupling between vehicle attitude and force generation. This paper proposes a robust control framework for an underactuated hexarotor equipped with a 1-DoF manipulator to perform sustained contact inspection. The architecture integrates integral-augmented Sliding Mode Control (SMC) for trajectory tracking with an admittance control law for force regulation. The contact force is mapped to a feedforward attitude term, while the 1-DoF arm actively compensates for the tilt to maintain surface alignment. Software-in-the-loop simulations demonstrate that the SMC-based approach achieves superior tracking and coupling rejection compared to traditional PID. Furthermore, the interaction strategy achieved precise force regulation with an RMSE of 0.12N and was able to stably exert up to 20N force, confirming the system's efficacy for stable, reliable contact-based inspection.

</details>


### [193] [TCAM for Autonomous Deformable Manipulation: The RMC2 Champion System for WBCD 2026 Track 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10718)
*Guangrui Shen,Zhili He,Shigang Wang,Yuanjun Sun,Qing Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍RMC2团队在WBCD 2026 Track 4（可变形操作挑战赛）中的冠军方案。团队基于TCAM（TermiBrain因果行动模型）框架构建了全自主系统，通过定制3D打印夹爪、腕部四相机感知方案、便携式UMI演示与真机演示结合的数据收集策略，以及闭环的因果分析-数据回补-策略微调机制，最终以平均约23秒/件的速度装载25件T恤，其中22件达到表面平滑要求，获得赛道第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比赛任务要求机器人在堆叠中取单件T恤、装载到印花托盘、对齐领口到目标区域并抚平印花区域，包含单层分离、可变形运输、精确定位和接触丰富的表面调整等复杂操作。比赛强烈激励全自主执行，因此需要开发一个能够在不需要人工干预的情况下完成整个任务序列的自主系统。

Method: 系统围绕TCAM框架构建，核心设计原则是让硬件、感知、数据和学习的联合设计共同降低策略需要处理的物理交互复杂度。具体包括：(1) 定制3D打印夹爪实现单层织物分离，在双臂ARX X5平台上提升拾取可靠性；(2) 腕部四相机设置，上方鱼眼相机提供任务级上下文，下方RGB相机提供近距夹爪-布料接触观测；(3) 结合便携式UMI风格演示与部署平台上的真机演示，提供广泛的操控先验和部署特化动力学；(4) TCAM将各组件闭环集成：分析每条轨迹的物理因素以识别结果成因，驱动针对性数据回补和策略微调；(5) 策略基于多视角VLA骨干，输出30步末端执行器增量位姿动作块。

Result: 在最终比赛中，系统以平均每次尝试约23秒的速度装载了25件T恤，其中22件达到所需的表面平滑度要求，在Track 4中荣获第一名。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了硬件-感知-数据-学习联合设计（而非仅依赖单一方面的改进）在解决可变形物体操控这类接触丰富任务中的有效性，TCAM的因果分析-数据回补闭环机制为任务失败提供了系统性的改进路径。

Abstract: This technical report describes the RMC2 Team's champion solution for the WBCD 2026 Track 4: Deformable Manipulation Challenge. The task requires a robot to pick a single T-shirt from a stack, load it onto a printing pallet, align the collar with a target area, and smooth the printing region, a sequence that involves single-layer separation, deformable transport, precise placement, and contact-rich surface adjustment. The competition strongly incentivizes fully autonomous execution, motivating the development of an autonomous solution. We built a fully autonomous system around the TCAM (TermiBrain Causal Action Model) framework, with the design principle that hardware, perception, data, and learning should jointly reduce the physical interaction complexity the policy must handle. A custom 3D-printed gripper designed for single-layer fabric separation improves picking reliability on a dual-arm ARX X5 platform. A wrist-centric four-camera setup pairs upper fisheye cameras for task-level context with lower RGB cameras for close-range gripper-cloth contact observation. We combine portable UMI-style demonstrations with real-robot demonstrations collected on the deployable platform to provide both broad manipulation priors and deployment-specific dynamics. TCAM ties these components into a closed loop: each trajectory is analyzed to identify the physical factors contributing to its outcome, driving targeted data recollection and policy fine-tuning. The policy outputs 30-step end-effector delta-pose action chunks from a multi-view VLA backbone. In the final competition, our system loaded 25 T-shirts at an average of approximately 23 seconds per attempt, with 22 achieving the required surface smoothness, securing first place in Track 4.

</details>


### [194] [Embodied Multimodal Grounding for Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation via Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10756)
*Huosen Ou,Dongni Song,Yuncong Wang,Tao Zhou,Yiding Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具身多模态接地框架，结合主动多视图语义3D高斯溅射（Semantic-3DGS）、可达性感知的基座定位和基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作策略，用于开放词汇目标接地的少样本操作。在50次真实机器人实验中，长程操作成功率60%，显著优于PointVLA（40%）和DexVLA（28%），并在高度变化、遮挡和杂乱场景下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身移动操作要求在执行前将语言、视觉观测、三维场景结构和动作可行性对齐。现有方法在杂乱、遮挡、视角变化和具身约束（如基座可达性）下缺乏鲁棒性，需要显式可刷新的3D语义接地来弥合语言理解与动作执行之间的差距。

Method: 提出一个具身多模态接地框架，核心组件包括：(1) 任务驱动的局部Semantic-3DGS作为共享接口，统一主动感知、语言条件化3D定位、障碍感知场景推理、基座准备和动作模型语义条件化；(2) 可达性感知的基座定位机制；(3) 扩散式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，将3D语义线索仅注入后期动作专家块以保留预训练动作先验。

Result: 在扩展的50次真实机器人实验中：(1) 长程操作成功率60%，优于PointVLA（40%）和DexVLA（28%）；(2) 重度杂乱操作中成功率74%，优于单视图变体（52%）和PointVLA（46%）；(3) 在75cm高度移位下保持75%成功率；(4) 消除了照片诱发的错误抓取。

Conclusion: 显式、可刷新的3D语义接地能够提升在杂乱、遮挡、视角变化和具身约束下的鲁棒性，验证了将3D语义感知与动作策略解耦注入的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [195] [JEPA-WAM: Stage-Level Joint-Embedding Prediction for World-Action Models in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10780)
*Xiao Liu,Yuguang Yang,Xi Wang,Kai Jiang,Cheng Chi,Yong Xu,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-WAM，通过Stage-JEPA预测阶段级语义未来（下一阶段的潜在目标），与短期物理未来互补，在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务中将成功率提升至90.25%，并减少5.97%的执行步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略通常将未来表示为固定的短视频-动作片段，只能捕捉局部场景演化以支持动作执行，但无法显式描述任务从当前阶段推进到下一阶段的阶段级未来。这种"短期物理未来"与"阶段级语义未来"的区分和互补是当前方法所缺失的。

Method: 在基于Motus的世界动作模型（WAM）基础上，引入Stage-JEPA——一种目标条件化的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）预测器。Stage-JEPA利用冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取当前状态表征，并预测下一推断阶段的潜在目标表示，从而将阶段级语义未来信息补充到世界动作模型中。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务（涵盖干净和随机化环境）中，JEPA-WAM达到90.25%的总体成功率，相比最强基线将成功rollout的平均执行步数减少5.97%。

Conclusion: 将短期物理未来与阶段级语义未来相结合的框架能够在提升任务成功率的同时提高执行效率，验证了显式建模任务阶段级进展对通用机器人策略的有效性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies aim to map multimodal observations and linguistic task instructions to actions across diverse tasks. However, existing methods typically represent the future as a fixed, short video-action chunk. This short-term future captures local scene evolution for action execution, but it does not explicitly describe the stage-level future that specifies how a task should progress from its current stage to the next. We therefore distinguish two complementary futures for robot manipulation: a short-term physical future to capture local scene evolution and a stage-level semantic future to represent task progress. We introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments a Motus-based World Action Model (WAM) with Stage-JEPA, a goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor. Given the current observation and task instruction, Stage-JEPA uses a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract the current-state representation and predicts the latent target of the next inferred stage. Across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks in clean and randomized environments, JEPA-WAM achieves 90.25% overall success and reduces the mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts by 5.97% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [196] [Dual Stress: Runtime Safety Monitoring for Safety-Constrained MPC Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10791)
*Jamil Chahine,Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型预测控制（MPC）优化中Karush-Kuhn-Tucker（KKT）乘子的"对偶应力信号"（dual stress signal）作为自主导航的危险性监测器，与传统的几何量（如间隙距离、碰撞时间、所需减速度）互补。在物理仿真器预注册的交叉场景中，该方法在匹配误报预算下，标记的漏检碰撞数量是全部几何检测器组合的4.7倍（85比18）；两者结合可对四分之三的可行刹车碰撞发出预警，而仅用几何检测器不到一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时危险监测器仅依赖于几何量（预计间隙、碰撞时间、所需减速度），忽略了模型预测控制器在每个控制步中通过约束优化天然产生的另一信息通道——KKT乘子。这些乘子衡量为维持对每个障碍物的安全而花费的边际控制力，作者试图评估其作为几何预警的补充信号的潜力。

Method: 对MPC约束优化产生的KKT乘子按预测时域加权求和，构造"对偶应力信号"作为危险指标。在预注册的held-out交叉场景中，通过物理仿真器驱动，将该信号与十五个几何检测器（在匹配的误报预算下调参）进行对比评估。

Result: 对偶应力信号标记了85个全部几何检测器遗漏的碰撞，而几何检测器只标记了18个应力信号遗漏的碰撞（比值4.7）。将两个通道结合后，能对四分之三的可行刹车碰撞发出预警，而单独使用几何检测器只能覆盖不到一半。

Conclusion: MPC优化中的对偶变量（KKT乘子）提供了与几何量互补的、可行动的运行时危险监测信号，二者结合可显著提升对可预防碰撞的预警覆盖率。

Abstract: Runtime hazard monitors for autonomous naviga- tion are conventionally built from geometric quantities: predicted clearance, time to collision, and required deceleration. A model-predictive controller that enforces safety through explicit con- straints computes, as a by-product of every control step, a second information channel that such monitors ignore: the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers of its constrained optimization, which measure the marginal control effort spent to maintain safety against each obstacle. This paper evaluates whether a horizon-weighted sum of those multipliers, a dual stress signal, provides a hazard monitor complementary to the geometric warnings the same state already supports. We compare it against a battery of fifteen geometric detectors tuned to a matched false-alarm budget, on preregistered held-out crossing scenarios driven through a physics simulator. The stress alarm actionably flags 4.7 times as many collisions missed by the entire geometric battery as the geometric battery flags in return (85 versus 18); combined, the two channels warn of three quarters of the collisions for which braking remained feasible, against under half for the geometric battery alone.

</details>


### [197] [AECNav: Active Evidence Consolidation for Efficient Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10817)
*Guanlin Liu,Shaobin Ling,Renyuan Liu,Zeying Gong,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AECNav，一个无需训练的零样本目标导航流水线，将ZSON重构为证据驱动的感知-决策问题，通过证据门控感知、证据整合和主动证据获取三个组件，在HM3D和MP3D基准上达到SOTA成功率（84.7%/57.3%/51.3%），并在四足机器人上实现95%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本开放词汇目标导航面临两大瓶颈：冗余感知管线导致高延迟，以及证据不足导致目标确认准确率有限。现有方法缺乏统一的语义基础，且难以将视觉上相似干扰物的虚假置信度与真实目标支持区分开来。

Method: AECNav采用三组件训练自由流水线：1) 证据门控感知——在所有推理阶段共享编码以建立统一语义基础，消除冗余计算；2) 证据整合——将检测结果聚合为簇级别的log-odds置信度信念，明确区分真实目标支持与相似干扰物的虚假置信度，并将目标预期缺失视为负证据；3) 主动证据获取——在弱语义线索下，通过选择以最小遍历成本最大化信息增益的前沿点来维持高效探索。

Result: 在HM3D-v2、HM3D-OVON和MP3D上分别达到84.7%、57.3%和51.3%的SOTA成功率，推理开销大幅降低；在物理四足机器人上以约5Hz频率实现40次试验95%的成功率。

Conclusion: AECNav通过将ZSON重构为证据驱动的感知-决策问题，证明了训练自由流水线在提升导航精度和效率方面的有效性，为开放词汇目标导航提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) in open-vocabulary scenarios is challenging, as it requires a robot to locate an arbitrarily specified object in an unseen environment without task-specific training. Currently, the task still suffers from high latency and limited accuracy due to redundant perception pipelines and insufficient evidence for reliable target confirmation. In this letter, we reframe ZSON as an evidence-driven perception-to-decision problem and present AECNav, a training-free pipeline built on three components: i) Evidence-gated perception, which utilizes a shared encoding across all reasoning stages to establish a unified semantic basis and eliminate redundant computations; ii) Evidence consolidation, which aggregates detections into cluster-level log-odds beliefs. This explicitly separates genuine target support from the false confidence of visually similar distractors, while treating the absence of expected detections as negative evidence; and iii) Active evidence acquisition, which sustains productive exploration under weak semantic cues by selecting frontiers that maximize information gain at minimal traversal cost. As a result, AECNav significantly outperforms previous methods and achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 84.7%, 57.3%, and 51.3% on HM3D-v2, HM3D-OVON, and MP3D, respectively, with substantially lower inference overhead, and attains 95% success across 40 trials on a physical quadruped robot at roughly 5Hz. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [198] [Neural Introspection Gating for Adaptive KV-Cache Reuse in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10824)
*Zhijie Wu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gated VLA-Cache，一种轻量级、免训练扩展方法，通过监测动作token预测的logit边际作为神经内省信号，在视觉相似性缓存基础上引入置信度门控，当置信度下降时触发完整重算，在四个LIBERO基准上恢复盲缓存造成的精度损失同时保留80%的计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在实时控制中反复计算相邻帧中几乎不变的视觉token的KV表示，造成大量冗余计算。现有方法如VLA-Cache虽然通过重用静态patch的KV状态来降低开销，但其策略仅依赖观测空间的启发式规则，未考虑模型自身的不确定性，导致某些场景下盲目缓存会损害策略可靠性。

Method: 提出Gated VLA-Cache：(1) 在视觉相似性缓存基础上增加神经内省机制；(2) 监测解码过程中前两个预测动作token之间的logit边际，作为零成本的置信度信号；(3) 当logit边际低于阈值时使缓存失效并触发完整重算；(4) 该方法为训练无关的轻量级扩展，可直接应用于OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT。

Result: 在四个LIBERO基准套件上采用OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT进行评估。在LIBERO-Goal和LIBERO-Long上，该方法恢复了超过100%因盲目缓存导致的精度损失，同时保留了80%的计算节省。

Conclusion: 通过将观测空间相似性启发式与模型内部的置信度信号相结合，Gated VLA-Cache在缓存计算节省与策略可靠性之间取得了更优的权衡，证明了神经内省是优化VLA模型实时推理效率的有效方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models map camera images and language instructions directly to motor commands through a single autoregressive transformer. In real-time control, they still spend substantial compute recomputing key-value(KV) representations for visual tokens that barely change across neighboring frames. Recent work such as VLA-Cache reduces that cost by reusing KV states for visually static patches, but its policy relies only on observation-space heuristics and does not account for the model's own uncertainty. We propose Gated VLA-Cache, a lightweight, training-free extension that augments visual-similarity caching with neural introspection. The method monitors the logit margin between the top two predicted action tokens, a zero-cost confidence signal available during decoding. When the margin drops below a threshold, the cache is invalidated and a full recompute is triggered. Evaluated on four LIBERO benchmark suites with both OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT, Gated VLA-Cache improves reliability when blind caching hurts. On LIBERO-Goal and LIBERO-Long, it recovers over 100% of the lost accuracy while retaining 80% of the compute savings.

</details>


### [199] [Enabling Scalable Kinesthetic Teaching via Observer-based Hand-guiding with Active Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10847)
*Anna Tuma,Giuseppe Monetti,Jochen J. Steil,Niels Dehio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RHOAS，一种基于模型力估计的机器人手引导方案，无需额外传感器即可主动支持操作者意图的运动，在KUKA LWR iiwa上的16人用户研究中显著降低了物理劳动强度并提高了可操纵性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动觉示教（kinesthetic teaching）通过机器人手引导为模仿学习和编程示范提供了自然界面，但长时间示教会导致操作者疲劳，降低示范质量并限制可扩展性。现有工业手引导方法通常不提供主动辅助，且替代方案需要昂贵的腕部力-矩传感器或依赖对新任务不可用的学习运动先验。

Method: RHOAS将机器人手引导视为操作者主动控制的交互而非被动环境交互。利用基于模型的外部力矩估计、内部关节力矩传感和冗余机器人运动学，在人体交互频率带宽内主动支持人的物理输入。解决基于观测器的力估计的实际挑战，包括抑制未建模的关节弹性动态效应和反馈路径中的测量噪声、接近运动学奇异点时的估计精度降低，以及静态重力补偿误差。

Result: 在KUKA LWR iiwa上进行16名参与者的用户研究，证明了在体力消耗方面有统计显著的降低，在精确和敏捷任务的机动性上有所改善，且用户偏好明确。

Conclusion: RHOAS证明了无需额外硬件即可通过模型基于力估计实现主动辅助的手引导，比传统被动式柔顺控制更有效，为示教学习和大规模数据采集提供了更可持续的方案。

Abstract: Kinesthetic teaching through robot hand-guiding provides a natural interface for collecting demonstrations in imitation learning and programming-by-demonstration. However, extended sessions cause operator fatigue, reducing demonstration quality and limiting scalability. Current industrial hand-guiding approaches typically provide no active assistance, and alternatives require costly wrist-mounted force-torque sensors or rely on learned motion priors unavailable for new tasks. We propose RHOAS, a hand-guiding scheme that actively supports operator-intended motions using model-based force estimation without additional hardware. Our approach considers robot hand-guiding as an actively controlled interaction by the human operator, rather than an interaction with a passive environment. Standard methods used for hand-guiding typically rely on general passivity-based compliant control architectures that unnecessarily increase operator effort and limit the range of demonstrable motions without providing the intended stability guarantees in active interaction. Instead, our design utilizes model-based external torque estimation, internal joint torque sensing, and redundant robot kinematics to actively support human physical input within the human interaction frequency bandwidth. We address practical challenges of relying on observer-based force estimation, including suppression of unmodeled joint elastic dynamic effects and measurement noise in the feedback path, reduced estimate accuracy close to kinematic singularities, and static gravity compensation errors. In a user study with 16 participants on a KUKA LWR iiwa we demonstrate statistically significant reductions in physical effort, improved maneuverability for both precise and agile tasks, and clear user preference.

</details>


### [200] [Flex-$π$: A Multi-Stream World-Action Model with Compute Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10860)
*Ge Yan,Jinghao Liu,Yuzhi Fan,Lei Cai,Minwen Liao,Jesse Zhang,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Flex-π，一个6B参数的世界动作模型（WAM），利用一个意外的"免费午餐"发现——冻结的视频生成VAE在编码RGB的同时几乎无损地编码3D点图，无需任何点图专用训练。这使得Flex-π能够在RGB之外同时以3D几何和DINO语义进行监督，在无需新增传感器、预训练或推理延迟的情况下提升策略性能。通过将视觉信号投影到共享潜在空间并与动作在Mixture-of-Transformers主干中联合去噪，并采用逐流dropout与跨模态强制技术，单个检查点可在任意流子集上运行（从纯动作快速模式到完整联合生成）。实验表明该策略在演示效率和泛化能力上表现优异，在灵巧、精确的真实世界双手操作任务中超越最强基线2-7倍，且运行速度快于π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）几乎全部仅预测RGB潜在表示，仅以像素重建为目标进行训练，缺乏对3D几何和物体语义操作所需的显式信号。作者希望在不增加新的传感器、预训练或推理开销的前提下，为WAM引入3D几何与语义监督。

Method: （1）发现冻结的视频生成VAE编码RGB时也能近无损编码3D点图，无需点图专用训练，从而"免费"获得3D几何监督；（2）构建Flex-π，6B参数WAM，将RGB、3D点图、DINO语义等所有视觉信号投影到共享潜在空间，与动作在Mixture-of-Transformers骨干中联合去噪；（3）采用逐流dropout与跨模态强制（per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing），使单一训练检查点可运行于任意流子集，支持从快速动作模式到完整联合生成模式的切换。

Result: Flex-π在灵巧、精确实时双手操作任务上，包括分布内与分布外场景，超越最强基线2-7倍，同时推理速度快于π0.5，展现出色的演示效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过利用共享视频VAE潜在空间中几何与语义信息的"免费午餐"，Flex-π证明了在不增加传感器、预训练和推理成本的前提下，为世界动作模型注入3D几何与语义监督能显著提升机器人操作策略的演示效率、泛化性能和推理速度，为WAM的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict the future to act better, but nearly all of them predict only RGB latents, trained purely for pixel reconstruction, with no explicit signal for the 3D geometry or object semantics manipulation needs. We find a surprising free lunch: the same frozen video-generation VAE that encodes RGB also encodes 3D pointmaps almost losslessly, with no pointmap-specific training at all. This lets us supervise Flex-$π$, a 6B-parameter WAM, on 3D geometry and object-centric DINO semantics alongside RGB, at no cost in new sensors, new pre-training, or inference latency. Every visual signal is projected into this shared latent space and denoised jointly with actions inside a Mixture-of-Transformers backbone; per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing then lets a single trained checkpoint run on any subset of these streams, from a fast action-only mode to full joint generation. The result is a policy that is exceptionally demonstration-efficient and generalizes well, beating the strongest baselines by up to 2-7$\times$ on dexterous, precise, real-world bimanual manipulation tasks both in and out of distribution, all while running faster than $π_{0.5}$. Our project website: https://flex-pi.github.io/

</details>


### [201] [Aerial Layouting: Design and Control of a Compliant and Actuated End-Effector for Precise In-flight Marking on Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10987)
*Christian Lanegger,Marco Ruggia,Marco Tognon,Lionel Ott,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型空中机器人系统，通过配备基于稳定性优化的Gough-Stewart机构的"智能"末端执行器，实现了毫米级精度的空中交互任务（如天花板画线），无需精确建模或复杂控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然空中机器人已在动态飞行等任务中实现厘米级精度，但在空中交互任务（如建筑施工放样、推滑操作）中需要毫米级定位精度，这是当前技术难以达到的。

Method: 设计了一种由飞行器搭载的"智能"末端执行器，基于稳定性优化的Gough-Stewart并联机构，融合了柔顺性、多接触点、驱动和自我包含四大设计原则。

Result: 通过实验验证了末端执行器的设计选择对于实现所需定位精度的必要性，并在实际中可靠地实现了天花板毫米级精度的画线任务，且无需精确建模或复杂的飞行器控制。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能末端执行器方案能够克服空中平台本身精度不足的限制，通过末端执行器层面的设计与补偿实现毫米级空中交互精度，为空中机器人在建筑施工等领域的高精度应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Aerial robots have demonstrated impressive feats of precise control, such as dynamic flight through openings or highly complex choreographies. Despite the accuracy needed for these tasks, there are problems that require levels of precision that are challenging to achieve today. One such problem is aerial interaction. Advances in aerial robot design and control have made such contact-based tasks possible and opened up research into challenging real-world tasks, including contact-based inspection. However, while centimetre accuracy is sufficient and achievable for inspection tasks, the positioning accuracy needed for other problems, such as layouting on construction sites or general push-and-slide tasks, is millimetres. To achieve such a high precision, we propose a new aerial system composed of an aerial vehicle equipped with a novel "smart" end-effector leveraging a stability-optimized Gough-Stewart mechanism. We present its design process and features incorporating the principles of compliance, multiple contact points, actuation, and self-containment. In experiments, we verify that the design choices made for our novel end-effector are necessary to obtain the desired positioning precision. Furthermore, we demonstrate that we can reliably mark lines on ceilings with millimetre accuracy without the need for precise modeling or sophisticated control of the aerial robot.

</details>


### [202] [Seeing above the waves: A modular sensing framework for data acquisition at sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10997)
*Jonathan E. Schmidt,Julius Wirbel,P. Nicholas Hansen,Morgan Louédec,Christian L. H. Westerdahl,Dimitrios Dagdilelis,Roberto Galeazzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向水面船舶自主导航的模块化、可复现传感器平台设计方案，集成了RADAR、LiDAR、IMU、GNSS、AIS、RGB/LWIR相机和气象传感器等多种模态，并配套ROS2软件框架实现数据管理、长期数据采集、硬件在环测试及与现有传感器和算法的集成，旨在通过统一硬件设计与数据捕获方法提升海事自主系统评估的可复现性和可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事自主航行需要对传感与感知子系统进行系统化评估，但海事环境具有独特挑战：传感器安装受船舶布局限制、雾气和海杂波等环境条件难以复现、长期任务使数据收集复杂化。现有方案缺乏模块化与可复现的标准化平台，因此需要回答如何设计一个模块化且可复现的海事自主传感器平台这一核心问题。

Method: 提出了一个综合性的设计蓝图：在硬件层面融合多种传感模态（RADAR、LiDAR、IMU、GNSS、AIS、RGB/LWIR相机、气象传感器）以增强环境感知与船舶本体感知；在软件层面构建基于ROS2的专用框架负责数据管理，支持长期数据采集、硬件在环测试以及与现有传感器和算法的集成；通过统一硬件设计与数据捕获方法论来提升跨船舶和研究项目间的可复现性与可比性。

Result: 该平台实现了硬件设计与数据采集方法的统一，增强了不同船舶和研究项目之间的可复现性与可比性，为推进态势感知和自主海事导航提供了标准化、可验证数据集的基础。

Conclusion: 该平台框架弥合了工程实现与研究方法的鸿沟，为标准化、可验证的数据集提供了基础，对推进态势感知和自主海事导航具有重要意义。

Abstract: Advancing autonomy for surface vessels requires systematic evaluation of their sensing and perception subsystems. Yet, maritime environments impose unique challenges: sensor installation is constrained by vessel layout, environmental conditions such as fog or sea clutter are difficult to reproduce, and long-duration missions complicate data collection. This work addresses the question: How can we design a modular and reproducible sensor platform for maritime autonomy?
  We present a comprehensive design blueprint that incorporates diverse modalities - RADAR, LiDAR, IMU, GNSS, AIS, RGB and LWIR cameras, and weather sensors - to enhance environmental awareness and vessel proprioception. Supported by a dedicated ROS2-based software framework for data management, our modular platform enables long-term data collection, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and integration with existing sensors and algorithms. By unifying hardware design and data capture methodology, the platform enhances reproducibility and comparability across vessels and research projects. The proposed framework bridges engineering implementation and research methodology, providing the foundation for standardized, verifiable datasets essential to advancing situational awareness and autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [203] [Deployment Is Not Destiny: Robot Recomposition in the Field with Unseen Software, Hardware, and Compute Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11063)
*Steven Swanbeck,Jonathan Salfity,Jeffery Gunawan,Corrie Van Sice,Mitch Pryor,Robert Blake Anderson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套运行时重组框架及其支撑抽象，使机器人能够在部署后通过即插即用方式快速集成新的模块化软件、硬件和计算负载，并将其共享给分布式对等节点，将重配置时间从数小时缩短至数分钟，无需开发者干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数机器人系统中子系统之间紧密耦合，虽然这是其复杂性的自然结果，但导致了单体式设计，这种设计耗时且难以在初始部署后适应变化。传统的手动集成需要数小时专家工作量，严重制约了机器人在现场场景中的快速适应能力。

Method: 提出了一个框架及配套的运行时重组抽象，支持真正的即插即用流程：允许非专家用户在野外快速添加新能力；新资源不仅立即可用于宿主机，还与分布式对等节点共享，使受计算约束的系统能够访问强大的远程能力。

Result: 该方法将重配置时间从传统数小时的专家人工集成缩短至数分钟且无需任何开发者干预，并在两个灾难响应场景中得到验证：在实际运行的核反应堆设施中进行放射性源定位，以及在黑暗、难以到达的空间中进行热引导的人员搜索。

Conclusion: 现场重组为动态需求提供了及时、灵活且可及的适应能力，是迈向可随任务、技术和环境快速演进的机器人的关键一步。

Abstract: The tight coupling of subsystems in most robots, though a natural consequence of their complexity, leads to monolithic designs that are time-consuming and difficult to adapt after initial deployment. To address this challenge, we present a framework and supporting abstractions for recomposition during runtime that enable robots to quickly integrate previously unseen modular software, hardware, and compute payloads. Our approach allows non-expert users to quickly add new capabilities in the field through a true plug-and-play process. Crucially, new resources are not only immediately available to a host robot but are also shared with distributed peers, enabling compute-constrained systems to access powerful new remote capabilities. Our framework reduces reconfiguration time to a matter of minutes with no developer intervention, in stark contrast to the hours of expert effort often required for traditional manual integration. We demonstrate our method in two disaster response scenarios, including radioactive source localization at an operational nuclear reactor facility and a thermal-guided search for people in dark, difficult-to-reach spaces. These demonstrations show how in-field recomposition provides timely, flexible, and accessible adaptation to dynamic requirements, representing a critical step toward creating robots that can quickly evolve alongside the tasks, technologies, and environments they support.

</details>


### [204] [VIScore: Diagnosing Planning-Relevant Quality in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11174)
*Haiyu Wu,Randall Balestriero,Morgan Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIScore指标，从编码器（Veracity）、预测器（Influence）和规划器（Sobriety）三个维度量化世界模型规划成功率，超越传统仅关注编码潜空间的指标（如straightness、empowerment等），在跨任务成功率池上实现超过0.75的Spearman相关，且是唯一在所有测试场景中校准误差低于常数拟合的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型规划中，将潜空间正则化为各向同性高斯分布虽能提供稳定且信息最大化的景观，但潜空间性质与规划成功率之间缺乏明确的联系。现有指标仅关注编码后的潜空间，无法充分解释规划成功率，因此需要更全面的度量指标。

Method: 首先对比SIGReg和VISReg两种正则化损失函数（相同分布目标但不同性质），发现VISReg更具控制灵活性且在大批量下更精确。基于此提出VIScore指标，从三个维度量化规划成功：(1) Veracity——编码特征的可达性；(2) Influence——基于编码特征的预测器容量；(3) Sobriety——基于搜索的规划器的幻觉程度。该指标同时覆盖编码器、预测器和规划器三个组件。

Result: 在seen和unseen模型及数据集上，VIScore在所有跨任务成功率池上Spearman相关系数持续超过0.75。与其他指标（straightness、physical-state probing、empowerment）相比，VIScore能更好地解释成功率；并且VIScore是唯一在所有测试场景中校准误差低于常数拟合的指标。

Conclusion: VIScore验证了编码器、预测器和规划器三个维度对规划成功的共同重要性，为世界模型设计和诊断提供了一个有效的度量工具，有望帮助未来相关研究。

Abstract: Regulating the latent space to an isotropic Gaussian distribution provides a stable and information-maximized landscape for world model planning. However, the latent space property and successful planning remain disconnected. We first study this by comparing SIGReg and VISReg, two regularization loss functions with the same distribution target but different properties. Compared with SIGReg, VISReg has more flexibility in controlling the weights of center, scale, and shape regularization, and a larger batch size brings a finer distribution approximation. We find that the former, despite being beneficial in self-supervised learning (SSL), does not help the planning, whereas the latter improves the planning success on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets. This motivates a deep understanding of the factors that correlate with the success rate. Unlike the previous metrics focusing on the encoded latent only, we propose the Veracity-Influence-Sobriety score (VIScore), a metric that quantifies the reachability and capacity of a predictor given the encoded feature, and the hallucination of the searching-based planner. Compared with straightness, physical-state probing, and empowerment, we show that, with the measurement covering encoder, predictor, and planner, VIScore explains the success rate better than the others, as reflected by a strong Spearman correlation. Specifically, VIScore consistently achieves a Spearman correlation over 0.75 on both seen and unseen models and datasets on the cross-task success rate pool. Moreover, VIScore is the only metric that has a calibration error below the constant fit across all testing scenarios, showcasing the importance of these three aspects in planning success. We hope this metric can help future studies on world model design and diagnosis.

</details>


### [205] [Risk-Aware Kinodynamic Motion Planning Under Uncertainty For Safe Navigation on Planetary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11175)
*Sachin Sunil Kelkar,Tanmay Dokania,Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段的风险感知运动规划框架：首先通过AO-RRT采样规划器生成动态可行、风险感知且渐近成本最优的轨迹，然后利用序列凸规划（SCP）将运动规划构造成非线性优化问题并以AO-RRT轨迹为初始解进行求解。通过条件风险价值（CVaR）量化风险，在仿真和硬件实验中风险降低超过97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
